Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the homologies space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 634 and the convergence rate is O(249/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_634(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 634. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^249(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on homologies structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 495. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 130 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 495 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the homologies structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 130. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
